// â”€â”€â”€ App Logo â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function LogoMark({ size = 36 }: { size?: number }) {
  return (
    <img src={logoMark} alt={APP_NAME}
      style={{ width: size, height: size, borderRadius: size * 0.22, objectFit: 'cover' }} />
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Header â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function Header({
  title, onBack, onMenu, showBell, theme, notif
}: {
  title?: string; onBack?: () => void; onMenu?: () => void
  showBell?: boolean; theme: 'dark' | 'light'; notif?: boolean
}) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 pt-4 pb-3 flex-shrink-0">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        {showBell && (
          <button className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center relative"
            style={{ background: t.surface }}>
            {Icons.bell(t.muted)}
            {notif && <span className="absolute top-1.5 right-1.5 w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#e8512a]" />}
          </button>
        )}
        {onMenu && (
          <button onClick={onMenu} className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center"
            style={{ background: t.surface }}>
            {Icons.menu(t.muted)}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      {title ? (
        <span className="text-base font-bold" style={{ color: t.text }}>{title}</span>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <LogoMark size={28} />
          <span className="text-base font-bold" style={{ color: t.text }}>{APP_NAME}</span>
        </div>
      )}
      {onBack ? (
        <button onClick={onBack} className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center"
          style={{ background: t.surface }}>
          {Icons.arrowRight(t.muted)}
        </button>
      ) : <div className="w-9" />}
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Bottom Nav â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function BottomNav({ active, onNav, theme }: { active: Screen; onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light' }) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  const tabs = [
    { s: 'contact' as Screen, label: 'ØªÙ…Ø§Ø³ Ø¨Ø§ Ù…Ø§', icon: (a: boolean) => Icons.phone(a ? '#00c896' : t.muted) },
    { s: 'settings' as Screen, label: 'ØªÙ†Ø¸ÛŒÙ…Ø§Øª', icon: (a: boolean) => Icons.settings(a ? '#00c896' : t.muted) },
    { s: 'home' as Screen, label: 'ØµÙØ­Ù‡ Ø§ØµÙ„ÛŒ', icon: (a: boolean) => Icons.home(a ? '#00c896' : t.muted) },
  ]
  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40"
      style={{ background: t.surface, borderTop: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-around py-2 px-4 max-w-sm mx-auto">
        {tabs.map(tab => (
          <button key={tab.s} onClick={() => onNav(tab.s)}
            className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1 px-4 py-1 rounded-xl transition-all">
            {tab.icon(active === tab.s)}
            <span className="text-[10px] font-medium"
              style={{ color: active === tab.s ? '#00c896' : t.muted }}>
              {tab.label}
            </span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Splash â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function SplashScreen({ onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState(0)
  useEffect(() => {
    const t1 = setTimeout(() => setPhase(1), 200)
    const t2 = setTimeout(() => setPhase(2), 900)
    const t3 = setTimeout(() => setPhase(3), 1600)
    const t4 = setTimeout(() => onDone(), 2400)
    return () => { clearTimeout(t1); clearTimeout(t2); clearTimeout(t3); clearTimeout(t4) }
  }, [onDone])

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden"
      style={{ background: '#050505' }}>
      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center pointer-events-none">
        <div className="w-80 h-80 rounded-full"
          style={{
            background: 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(0,200,150,0.08) 0%, transparent 70%)',
            opacity: phase >= 1 ? 1 : 0,
            transition: 'opacity 0.8s ease',
          }} />
      </div>

      <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-center"
        style={{
          opacity: phase >= 1 ? 1 : 0,
          transform: phase >= 1 ? 'scale(1) translateY(0)' : 'scale(0.75) translateY(20px)',
          transition: 'all 0.8s cubic-bezier(0.34,1.2,0.64,1)',
        }}>
        <img src={logoFull} alt={APP_NAME}
          style={{ width: 220, height: 220, objectFit: 'contain' }} />
      </div>

      <div className="relative z-10 mt-2"
        style={{
          opacity: phase >= 2 ? 1 : 0,
          transform: phase >= 2 ? 'translateY(0)' : 'translateY(12px)',
          transition: 'all 0.5s ease',
        }}>
        <p className="text-sm font-medium tracking-wide" style={{ color: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.7)' }}>
          Ú©ÛŒÙ Ù¾ÙˆÙ„ Ø¯ÛŒØ¬ÛŒØªØ§Ù„ Ø´Ù…Ø§
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="absolute bottom-14 flex flex-col items-center gap-3"
        style={{ opacity: phase >= 3 ? 1 : 0, transition: 'all 0.4s ease' }}>
        <div className="flex gap-1.5">
          {[0, 1, 2].map(i => (
            <div key={i} className="rounded-full bg-[#00c896]"
              style={{ width: i === 1 ? '18px' : '6px', height: '6px', opacity: 0.4 + i * 0.3 }} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Login â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function LoginScreen({ onLogin }: { onLogin: (p: string) => void }) {
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
  const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    if (phone.length < 10 || loading) return
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      await apiCall('/api/auth/send-otp', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ phone }) })
    } catch {}
    setLoading(false)
    onLogin(phone)
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden" style={{ background: '#050505', height: '100dvh' }}>
      {/* Logo */}
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-8 pb-3 px-6 flex-shrink-0">
        <img src={logoFull} alt={APP_NAME}
          style={{ width: 120, height: 120, objectFit: 'contain' }} />
        <p className="text-[#00c896] text-xs font-medium tracking-wide mt-1">Ú©ÛŒÙ Ù¾ÙˆÙ„ Ø¯ÛŒØ¬ÛŒØªØ§Ù„</p>
      </div>

      <div className="h-px mx-8 flex-shrink-0" style={{ background: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.12)' }} />

      {/* Form */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col px-5 pt-5 pb-5 overflow-hidden">
        <h2 className="text-white text-sm font-semibold text-right mb-3 flex-shrink-0">Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡ Ù‡Ù…Ø±Ø§Ù‡ Ø®ÙˆØ¯ Ø±Ø§ ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ú©Ù†ÛŒØ¯</h2>

        <div className="mb-3 flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 rounded-2xl h-13 transition-all"
            style={{
              height: '52px',
              background: '#1a1a1a',
              border: `2px solid ${focused ? '#00c896' : '#2e2e2e'}`,
              boxShadow: focused ? '0 0 0 4px rgba(0,200,150,0.08)' : 'none',
            }}>
            <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0" style={{ background: '#1e3030' }}>
              {Icons.mobile('#00c896')}
            </div>
            <input type="tel" value={phone}
              onChange={e => setPhone(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 11))}
              onFocus={() => setFocused(true)} onBlur={() => setFocused(false)}
              onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && handleSubmit()}
              placeholder="09xxxxxxxxx" dir="ltr"
              className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-white placeholder-[#444] text-base outline-none text-right"
              style={{ fontFamily: 'Vazirmatn, sans-serif' }} />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-xl mb-3 flex-shrink-0"
          style={{ background: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.06)', border: '1px solid rgba(0,200,150,0.12)' }}>
          {Icons.info('#00c896')}
          <p className="text-[#6b9e8e] text-xs text-right flex-1">Ú©Ø¯ ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡ Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØ´ÙˆØ¯</p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 min-h-0" />

        <p className="text-[#444] text-xs text-center mb-3 leading-relaxed flex-shrink-0">
          ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯ Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…Ø¹Ù†Ø§ÛŒ Ù¾Ø°ÛŒØ±Ø´ <span className="text-[#00c896]">Ù‚ÙˆØ§Ù†ÛŒÙ†</span> Ùˆ <span className="text-[#00c896]">Ø­Ø±ÛŒÙ… Ø®ØµÙˆØµÛŒ</span> {APP_NAME} Ø§Ø³Øª
        </p>
        <button onClick={handleSubmit}
          disabled={phone.length < 10 || loading}
          className="w-full rounded-2xl text-white font-bold text-sm transition-all flex-shrink-0"
          style={{
            height: '52px',
            background: phone.length >= 10 && !loading
              ? 'linear-gradient(135deg, #e8512a, #c93e1a)'
              : '#1a1a1a',
            color: phone.length >= 10 && !loading ? 'white' : '#444',
          }}>
          {loading ? 'Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„...' : 'Ø§Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù‡'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ OTP â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function OtpScreen({ phone, onVerify }: { phone: string; onVerify: (code: string) => void }) {
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState(['', '', '', '', ''])
  const [timer, setTimer] = useState(90)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const inputRefs = useRef<(HTMLInputElement | null)[]>([null, null, null, null, null])

  useEffect(() => { inputRefs.current[0]?.focus() }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    const iv = setInterval(() => setTimer(x => x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0), 1000)
    return () => clearInterval(iv)
  }, [])

  const handleChange = (i: number, v: string) => {
    if (!/^\d*$/.test(v)) return
    const digit = v.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-1)
    const next = [...otp]
    next[i] = digit
    setOtp(next)
    if (digit && i < 4) inputRefs.current[i + 1]?.focus()
    if (next.every(d => d)) setTimeout(() => onVerify(next.join('')), 300)
  }

  const handleKeyDown = (i: number, e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Backspace' && !otp[i] && i > 0) {
      const next = [...otp]; next[i - 1] = ''
      setOtp(next)
      inputRefs.current[i - 1]?.focus()
    }
  }

  const handleResend = async () => {
    setTimer(90)
    try { await apiCall('/api/auth/send-otp', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ phone }) }) } catch {}
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden" style={{ background: '#050505', height: '100dvh' }}>
      <Header title="ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡" onBack={() => {}} theme="dark" />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col px-5 pt-4 pb-6 overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center mb-6 flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mb-3"
            style={{ background: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.08)', border: '1px solid rgba(0,200,150,0.15)' }}>
            {Icons.phone('#00c896')}
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-white text-base font-bold mb-1">Ú©Ø¯ ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø´Ø¯</h2>
          <p className="text-[#555] text-sm text-center">
            Ú©Ø¯ Ûµ Ø±Ù‚Ù…ÛŒ Ø¨Ù‡ <span className="text-[#00c896]" dir="ltr">{phone}</span> Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ø´Ø¯
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-2.5 justify-center mb-5 flex-shrink-0" dir="ltr">
          {otp.map((d, i) => (
            <input key={i}
              ref={el => { inputRefs.current[i] = el }}
              type="tel" inputMode="numeric" value={d}
              onChange={e => handleChange(i, e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={e => handleKeyDown(i, e)}

          ))}

          {loading ? 'Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ Ø¨Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒ...' : 'ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ùˆ ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯'}

      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function DropdownMenu({ onClose, onNav, theme }: { onClose: () => void; onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light' }) {

    { label: 'Ù¾Ø±ÙˆÙØ§ÛŒÙ„', icon: Icons.user(t.muted), s: 'home' as Screen },

        style={{

const WALLET_ADDRESS = '0x39d8d946d1b9698ca166b21d805ee94f969a188d'

function ReceiveScreen({ phone, onNav, theme }: { phone: string; onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light' }) {

    navigator.clipboard.writeText(val).catch(() => {})

          style={{ background: copied === copyKey ? 'rgba(0,200,150,0.15)' : t.card }}>

  )

// â”€â”€â”€ Transfer â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function TransferScreen({ onNav, theme, usdtPrice, balance }: { onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light'; usdtPrice: number; balance: Balance }) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1)
  const [srcCurrency, setSrcCurrency] = useState<Currency>('rial')
  const [destType, setDestType] = useState<DestType>('kelid')
  const [destValue, setDestValue] = useState('')
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState('')
  const [destCurrency, setDestCurrency] = useState<Currency>('rial')
  const [kelidStatus, setKelidStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'checking' | 'found' | 'notfound'>('idle')
  const [warnAccepted, setWarnAccepted] = useState(false)
  const [paymentId] = useState(() => Math.floor(1000000 + Math.random() * 9000000).toString())
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false)
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)

  const COMMISSION = 0.003
  const NET_FEE_USDT = 1
  const amountNum = parseFloat(amount.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) || 0

  const computeFees = () => {
    if (srcCurrency === 'rial' && destCurrency === 'rial') {
      const fee = Math.round(amountNum * COMMISSION)
      return { deductRial: amountNum + fee, depositRial: amountNum, depositUsdt: 0, feeRial: fee, feeUsdt: 0, netFee: 0 }
    }
    if (srcCurrency === 'usdt' && destCurrency === 'usdt') {
      const fee = amountNum * COMMISSION
      return { deductRial: 0, depositRial: 0, depositUsdt: amountNum - fee - NET_FEE_USDT, feeRial: 0, feeUsdt: fee, netFee: NET_FEE_USDT }
    }
    if (srcCurrency === 'rial' && destCurrency === 'usdt') {
      const usdt = amountNum / usdtPrice
      const fee = usdt * COMMISSION
      return { deductRial: amountNum, depositRial: 0, depositUsdt: usdt - fee - NET_FEE_USDT, feeRial: 0, feeUsdt: fee, netFee: NET_FEE_USDT }
    }
    const rial = amountNum * usdtPrice
    const fee = rial * COMMISSION
    return { deductRial: 0, depositRial: rial - fee, depositUsdt: 0, feeRial: fee, feeUsdt: 0, netFee: 0 }
  }
  const fees = computeFees()

  const checkKelid = useCallback(async () => {
    if (destType !== 'kelid' || !destValue) return
    setKelidStatus('checking')
    try {
      const d = await apiCall(`/api/users/lookup?phone=${destValue}`)
      setKelidStatus(d.found ? 'found' : 'notfound')
    } catch {
      setKelidStatus(KNOWN_USERS.includes(destValue) ? 'found' : 'notfound')
    }
  }, [destType, destValue])

  const handleTransfer = async () => {
    if (amountNum <= 0 || submitting) return
    setSubmitting(true)
    try {
      const d = await apiCall('/api/transfer', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ srcCurrency, destType, destValue, amount: amountNum, destCurrency, paymentId }),
      })
      if (d.success || d.id) { setDone(true); return }
    } catch {}
    setDone(true)
    setSubmitting(false)
  }

  if (done) return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen items-center justify-center px-5 animate-scalein" style={{ background: t.bg }}>
      <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-5"
        style={{ background: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.12)' }}>
        {Icons.check('#00c896')}
      </div>
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: t.text }}>Ø§Ù†ØªÙ‚Ø§Ù„ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚</h2>
      <p className="text-center text-sm mb-2" style={{ color: t.muted }}>
        {srcCurrency === 'rial' ? `${amountNum.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} Ø±ÛŒØ§Ù„` : `${amountNum} USDT`} Ù…Ù†ØªÙ‚Ù„ Ø´Ø¯
      </p>
      <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl text-center mt-2 mb-4" style={{ background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
        <p className="text-xs mb-1" style={{ color: t.muted }}>Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ù‡ Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø®Øª</p>
        <p className="text-[#00c896] font-mono text-xl font-bold">{paymentId}</p>
      </div>
      <button onClick={() => onNav('home')} className="w-full rounded-2xl text-white font-bold mt-4"
        style={{ height: '52px', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #e8512a, #c93e1a)' }}>
        Ø¨Ø§Ø²Ú¯Ø´Øª Ø¨Ù‡ Ø®Ø§Ù†Ù‡
      </button>
    </div>
  )

  const usdtBal = balance.loaded ? balance.usdt.toLocaleString('fa-IR', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) : '---'

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen" style={{ background: t.bg }}>
      <Header title="Ø§Ù†ØªÙ‚Ø§Ù„ ÙˆØ¬Ù‡" onBack={() => step > 1 ? setStep(s => s - 1) : onNav('home')} theme={theme} />
      <div className="flex gap-1.5 px-5 mb-4">
        {[1, 2, 3].map(s => (
          <div key={s} className="flex-1 h-1 rounded-full transition-all"
            style={{ background: step >= s ? '#00c896' : (theme === 'dark' ? '#2a2a2a' : '#ddd') }} />
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 pb-28 animate-fadein">
        {step === 1 && (
          <>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold mb-3 text-right" style={{ color: t.text }}>Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ù…Ø¨Ø¯Ø£</h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 mb-5">
              {([['rial', 'Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ø±ÛŒØ§Ù„ÛŒ', `${rialBal} Ø±ÛŒØ§Ù„`], ['usdt', 'Ú©ÛŒÙ ØªØªØ±', `${usdtBal} USDT`]] as const).map(([cur, label, bal]) => (

// â”€â”€â”€ Transactions â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function TransactionsScreen({ onNav, theme }: { onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light' }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<'all' | 'in' | 'out'>('all')
  const [txList, setTxList] = useState<Transaction[]>(FALLBACK_TRANSACTIONS)
  const t = useTheme(theme)

  useEffect(() => {
    apiCall('/api/transactions')
      .then(d => { if (d.transactions?.length > 0) setTxList(d.transactions) })
      .catch(() => {})
  }, [])

  const filtered = txList.filter(tx => filter === 'all' || tx.type === filter)

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen" style={{ background: t.bg }}>
      <Header title="ØªØ±Ø§Ú©Ù†Ø´â€ŒÙ‡Ø§" onBack={() => onNav('home')} theme={theme} />
      <div className="flex gap-2 px-4 mb-4">
        {[['all', 'Ù‡Ù…Ù‡'], ['in', 'Ø¯Ø±ÛŒØ§ÙØªÛŒ'], ['out', 'Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø®ØªÛŒ']].map(([k, l]) => (
          <button key={k} onClick={() => setFilter(k as typeof filter)}
            className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-all"
            style={{ background: filter === k ? '#00c896' : t.surface, color: filter === k ? 'white' : t.muted, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
            {l}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 pb-24">
        {filtered.map(tx => (
          <div key={tx.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-2xl mb-2"
            style={{ background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0"
              style={{ background: tx.type === 'in' ? 'rgba(0,200,150,0.12)' : 'rgba(232,81,42,0.12)' }}>
              {tx.type === 'in' ? Icons.receive('#00c896') : Icons.transfer('#e8512a')}
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium truncate text-right" style={{ color: t.text }}>{tx.title}</p>
              <p className="text-xs truncate text-right mt-0.5" style={{ color: t.muted }}>{tx.subtitle}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: tx.type === 'in' ? '#00c896' : '#e8512a' }}>
                {tx.type === 'in' ? '+' : '-'}{tx.amount} {tx.currency === 'usdt' ? 'USDT' : 'Ø±ÛŒØ§Ù„'}
              </p>

function SettingsScreen({ onNav, theme, setTheme, notif, setNotif }: { onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light'; setTheme: (t: 'dark' | 'light') => void; notif: boolean; setNotif: (v: boolean) => void }) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  const Toggle = ({ value, onChange }: { value: boolean; onChange: (v: boolean) => void }) => (
    <button onClick={() => onChange(!value)} className="w-12 h-6 rounded-full transition-all relative flex-shrink-0"
      style={{ background: value ? '#00c896' : (theme === 'dark' ? '#333' : '#ccc') }}>
      <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-white absolute top-0.5 transition-all"
        style={{ left: value ? '26px' : '2px', boxShadow: '0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.3)' }} />
    </button>
  )
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen" style={{ background: t.bg }}>
      <Header title="ØªÙ†Ø¸ÛŒÙ…Ø§Øª" onBack={() => onNav('home')} theme={theme} />
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 pb-24">
        <p className="text-xs font-medium mb-2 text-right" style={{ color: t.muted }}>Ø¸Ø§Ù‡Ø±</p>
        <div className="rounded-2xl mb-4" style={{ background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-4">
            <Toggle value={theme === 'dark'} onChange={v => setTheme(v ? 'dark' : 'light')} />
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="text-right">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium" style={{ color: t.text }}>ØªÙ… ØªØ§Ø±ÛŒÚ©</p>
                <p className="text-xs" style={{ color: t.muted }}>{theme === 'dark' ? 'ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„' : 'ØºÛŒØ±ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„'}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center" style={{ background: theme === 'dark' ? '#2a2a2a' : '#f0f0f0' }}>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '18px' }}>{theme === 'dark' ? 'ğŸŒ™' : 'â˜€ï¸'}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs font-medium mb-2 text-right" style={{ color: t.muted }}>Ø§Ø¹Ù„Ø§Ù†â€ŒÙ‡Ø§</p>
        <div className="rounded-2xl" style={{ background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>

            <Toggle value={notif} onChange={setNotif} />
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="text-right">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium" style={{ color: t.text }}>Ø§Ø¹Ù„Ø§Ù†â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ ØªØ±Ø§Ú©Ù†Ø´</p>

// â”€â”€â”€ Root â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export default function App() {
  const [screen, setScreen] = useState<Screen>('splash')
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<'dark' | 'light'>('dark')
  const [notif, setNotif] = useState(true)
  const [usdtPrice, setUsdtPrice] = useState(700000)
  const [balance, setBalance] = useState<Balance>({ rial: 0, usdt: 0, loaded: false })

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchPrice = async () => {
      try {
        const d = await apiCall('/api/price/usdt')
        if (d.price) setUsdtPrice(d.price)
      } catch {
        try {
          const r = await fetch('https://api.nobitex.ir/market/stats?srcCurrency=usdt&dstCurrency=rls')
          const d = await r.json()
          const p = d?.stats?.['usdt-rls']?.lastTradePrice
          if (p) setUsdtPrice(parseFloat(p))
        } catch {}
      }
    }
    fetchPrice()
    const iv = setInterval(fetchPrice, 30000)
    return () => clearInterval(iv)
  }, [])

  const fetchBalance = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const d = await apiCall('/api/wallet/balance')
      if (d.rial_balance !== undefined) {
        setBalance({ rial: Number(d.rial_balance), usdt: parseFloat(d.usdt_balance || '0'), loaded: true })
      }
    } catch {
      setBalance(b => ({ ...b, loaded: true }))
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => { if (screen === 'home') fetchBalance() }, [screen, fetchBalance])

  const nav = (s: Screen) => setScreen(s)

  const handleLogin = (p: string) => { setPhone(p); nav('otp') }

  const handleVerify = async (code: string) => {
    try {
      const d = await apiCall('/api/auth/verify-otp', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ phone, code }),
      })
      if (d.token) localStorage.setItem('anpardaz_token', d.token)
    } catch {}
    nav('home')
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-sm mx-auto relative" style={{ minHeight: '100vh', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
      {screen === 'splash' && <SplashScreen onDone={() => nav('login')} />}
      {screen === 'login' && <LoginScreen onLogin={handleLogin} />}
      {screen === 'otp' && <OtpScreen phone={phone} onVerify={handleVerify} />}
      {screen === 'home' && <HomeScreen phone={phone || '09137654321'} onNav={nav} theme={theme} notif={notif} balance={balance} />}
      {screen === 'transfer' && <TransferScreen onNav={nav} theme={theme} usdtPrice={usdtPrice} balance={balance} />}
      {screen === 'receive' && <ReceiveScreen phone={phone || '09137654321'} onNav={nav} theme={theme} />}
      {screen === 'transactions' && <TransactionsScreen onNav={nav} theme={theme} />}
      {screen === 'internet' && <InternetScreen onNav={nav} theme={theme} />}
      {screen === 'settings' && <SettingsScreen onNav={nav} theme={theme} setTheme={setTheme} notif={notif} setNotif={setNotif} />}
      {screen === 'addcard' && <AddCardScreen onNav={nav} theme={theme} />}
      {screen === 'contact' && <ContactScreen onNav={nav} theme={theme} />}
    </div>
  )
}

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react'
import logoMark from './imports/25087b17-7744-4c8c-b6b5-85499fc1d7bc.jpg'

// â”€â”€â”€ API â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://78.157.51.76/api'
const APP_NAME = 'Ø¢Ù† Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø²'

async function apiCall(path: string, opts: RequestInit = {}) {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('anpardaz_token')
  const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    ...opts,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      ...(opts.headers as Record<string, string> || {}),
    },
  })
  return res.json()
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Theme helpers â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function useTheme(theme: 'dark' | 'light') {
  return {
    bg: theme === 'dark' ? '#0f0f0f' : '#f2f4f7',
    surface: theme === 'dark' ? '#1a1a1a' : '#ffffff',
    card: theme === 'dark' ? '#222222' : '#ffffff',
    border: theme === 'dark' ? '#2e2e2e' : '#e2e6ea',
    text: theme === 'dark' ? '#f0f0f0' : '#111827',
    muted: theme === 'dark' ? '#888888' : '#6b7280',
    sub: theme === 'dark' ? '#555' : '#9ca3af',
  }
}

// â”€â”€â”€ App Logo â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function LogoMark({ size = 36 }: { size?: number }) {
  return (
    <img src={logoMark} alt={APP_NAME}
      style={{ width: size, height: size, borderRadius: size * 0.22, objectFit: 'cover' }} />
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Header â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function Header({
  title, onBack, onMenu, showBell, theme, notif
}: {
  title?: string; onBack?: () => void; onMenu?: () => void
  showBell?: boolean; theme: 'dark' | 'light'; notif?: boolean
}) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 pt-4 pb-3 flex-shrink-0">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        {showBell && (
          <button className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center relative"
            style={{ background: t.surface }}>
            {Icons.bell(t.muted)}
            {notif && <span className="absolute top-1.5 right-1.5 w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#e8512a]" />}
          </button>
        )}
        {onMenu && (
          <button onClick={onMenu} className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center"
            style={{ background: t.surface }}>
            {Icons.menu(t.muted)}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      {title ? (
        <span className="text-base font-bold" style={{ color: t.text }}>{title}</span>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <LogoMark size={28} />
          <span className="text-base font-bold" style={{ color: t.text }}>{APP_NAME}</span>
        </div>
      )}
      {onBack ? (
        <button onClick={onBack} className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center"
          style={{ background: t.surface }}>
          {Icons.arrowRight(t.muted)}
        </button>
      ) : <div className="w-9" />}
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Bottom Nav â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function BottomNav({ active, onNav, theme }: { active: Screen; onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light' }) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  const tabs = [
    { s: 'contact' as Screen, label: 'ØªÙ…Ø§Ø³ Ø¨Ø§ Ù…Ø§', icon: (a: boolean) => Icons.phone(a ? '#00c896' : t.muted) },
    { s: 'settings' as Screen, label: 'ØªÙ†Ø¸ÛŒÙ…Ø§Øª', icon: (a: boolean) => Icons.settings(a ? '#00c896' : t.muted) },
    { s: 'home' as Screen, label: 'ØµÙØ­Ù‡ Ø§ØµÙ„ÛŒ', icon: (a: boolean) => Icons.home(a ? '#00c896' : t.muted) },
  ]
  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40"
      style={{ background: t.surface, borderTop: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-around py-2 px-4 max-w-sm mx-auto">
        {tabs.map(tab => (
          <button key={tab.s} onClick={() => onNav(tab.s)}
            className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1 px-4 py-1 rounded-xl transition-all">
            {tab.icon(active === tab.s)}
            <span className="text-[10px] font-medium"
              style={{ color: active === tab.s ? '#00c896' : t.muted }}>
              {tab.label}
            </span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Splash â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function SplashScreen({ onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState(0)
  useEffect(() => {
    const t1 = setTimeout(() => setPhase(1), 200)
    const t2 = setTimeout(() => setPhase(2), 900)
    const t3 = setTimeout(() => setPhase(3), 1600)
    const t4 = setTimeout(() => onDone(), 2400)
    return () => { clearTimeout(t1); clearTimeout(t2); clearTimeout(t3); clearTimeout(t4) }
  }, [onDone])

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden"
      style={{ background: '#050505' }}>
      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center pointer-events-none">
        <div className="w-80 h-80 rounded-full"
          style={{
            background: 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(0,200,150,0.08) 0%, transparent 70%)',
            opacity: phase >= 1 ? 1 : 0,
            transition: 'opacity 0.8s ease',
          }} />
      </div>

      <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-center"
        style={{
          opacity: phase >= 1 ? 1 : 0,
          transform: phase >= 1 ? 'scale(1) translateY(0)' : 'scale(0.75) translateY(20px)',
          transition: 'all 0.8s cubic-bezier(0.34,1.2,0.64,1)',
        }}>
        <img src={logoFull} alt={APP_NAME}
          style={{ width: 220, height: 220, objectFit: 'contain' }} />
      </div>

      <div className="relative z-10 mt-2"
        style={{
          opacity: phase >= 2 ? 1 : 0,
          transform: phase >= 2 ? 'translateY(0)' : 'translateY(12px)',
          transition: 'all 0.5s ease',
        }}>
        <p className="text-sm font-medium tracking-wide" style={{ color: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.7)' }}>
          Ú©ÛŒÙ Ù¾ÙˆÙ„ Ø¯ÛŒØ¬ÛŒØªØ§Ù„ Ø´Ù…Ø§
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="absolute bottom-14 flex flex-col items-center gap-3"
        style={{ opacity: phase >= 3 ? 1 : 0, transition: 'all 0.4s ease' }}>
        <div className="flex gap-1.5">
          {[0, 1, 2].map(i => (
            <div key={i} className="rounded-full bg-[#00c896]"
              style={{ width: i === 1 ? '18px' : '6px', height: '6px', opacity: 0.4 + i * 0.3 }} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Login â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function LoginScreen({ onLogin }: { onLogin: (p: string) => void }) {
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
  const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    if (phone.length < 10 || loading) return
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      await apiCall('/api/auth/send-otp', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ phone }) })
    } catch {}
    setLoading(false)
    onLogin(phone)
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden" style={{ background: '#050505', height: '100dvh' }}>
      {/* Logo */}
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-8 pb-3 px-6 flex-shrink-0">
        <img src={logoFull} alt={APP_NAME}
          style={{ width: 120, height: 120, objectFit: 'contain' }} />
        <p className="text-[#00c896] text-xs font-medium tracking-wide mt-1">Ú©ÛŒÙ Ù¾ÙˆÙ„ Ø¯ÛŒØ¬ÛŒØªØ§Ù„</p>
      </div>

      <div className="h-px mx-8 flex-shrink-0" style={{ background: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.12)' }} />

      {/* Form */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col px-5 pt-5 pb-5 overflow-hidden">
        <h2 className="text-white text-sm font-semibold text-right mb-3 flex-shrink-0">Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡ Ù‡Ù…Ø±Ø§Ù‡ Ø®ÙˆØ¯ Ø±Ø§ ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ú©Ù†ÛŒØ¯</h2>

        <div className="mb-3 flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 rounded-2xl h-13 transition-all"
            style={{
              height: '52px',
              background: '#1a1a1a',
              border: `2px solid ${focused ? '#00c896' : '#2e2e2e'}`,
              boxShadow: focused ? '0 0 0 4px rgba(0,200,150,0.08)' : 'none',
            }}>
            <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0" style={{ background: '#1e3030' }}>
              {Icons.mobile('#00c896')}
            </div>
            <input type="tel" value={phone}
              onChange={e => setPhone(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 11))}
              onFocus={() => setFocused(true)} onBlur={() => setFocused(false)}
              onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && handleSubmit()}
              placeholder="09xxxxxxxxx" dir="ltr"
              className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-white placeholder-[#444] text-base outline-none text-right"
              style={{ fontFamily: 'Vazirmatn, sans-serif' }} />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-xl mb-3 flex-shrink-0"
          style={{ background: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.06)', border: '1px solid rgba(0,200,150,0.12)' }}>
          {Icons.info('#00c896')}
          <p className="text-[#6b9e8e] text-xs text-right flex-1">Ú©Ø¯ ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡ Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØ´ÙˆØ¯</p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 min-h-0" />

        <p className="text-[#444] text-xs text-center mb-3 leading-relaxed flex-shrink-0">
          ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯ Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…Ø¹Ù†Ø§ÛŒ Ù¾Ø°ÛŒØ±Ø´ <span className="text-[#00c896]">Ù‚ÙˆØ§Ù†ÛŒÙ†</span> Ùˆ <span className="text-[#00c896]">Ø­Ø±ÛŒÙ… Ø®ØµÙˆØµÛŒ</span> {APP_NAME} Ø§Ø³Øª
        </p>
        <button onClick={handleSubmit}
          disabled={phone.length < 10 || loading}
          className="w-full rounded-2xl text-white font-bold text-sm transition-all flex-shrink-0"
          style={{
            height: '52px',
            background: phone.length >= 10 && !loading
              ? 'linear-gradient(135deg, #e8512a, #c93e1a)'
              : '#1a1a1a',
            color: phone.length >= 10 && !loading ? 'white' : '#444',
          }}>
          {loading ? 'Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„...' : 'Ø§Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù‡'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ OTP â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function OtpScreen({ phone, onVerify }: { phone: string; onVerify: (code: string) => void }) {
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState(['', '', '', '', ''])
  const [timer, setTimer] = useState(90)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const inputRefs = useRef<(HTMLInputElement | null)[]>([null, null, null, null, null])

  useEffect(() => { inputRefs.current[0]?.focus() }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    const iv = setInterval(() => setTimer(x => x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0), 1000)
    return () => clearInterval(iv)
  }, [])

  const handleChange = (i: number, v: string) => {
    if (!/^\d*$/.test(v)) return
    const digit = v.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-1)
    const next = [...otp]
    next[i] = digit
    setOtp(next)
    if (digit && i < 4) inputRefs.current[i + 1]?.focus()
    if (next.every(d => d)) setTimeout(() => onVerify(next.join('')), 300)
  }

  const handleKeyDown = (i: number, e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Backspace' && !otp[i] && i > 0) {
      const next = [...otp]; next[i - 1] = ''
      setOtp(next)
      inputRefs.current[i - 1]?.focus()
    }
  }

  const handleResend = async () => {
    setTimer(90)
    try { await apiCall('/api/auth/send-otp', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ phone }) }) } catch {}
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden" style={{ background: '#050505', height: '100dvh' }}>
      <Header title="ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡" onBack={() => {}} theme="dark" />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col px-5 pt-4 pb-6 overflow-hidden">

// â”€â”€â”€ Dropdown Menu â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function DropdownMenu({ onClose, onNav, theme }: { onClose: () => void; onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light' }) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  const items = [
    { label: 'Ù¾Ø±ÙˆÙØ§ÛŒÙ„', icon: Icons.user(t.muted), s: 'home' as Screen },

    { label: 'ØªØ±Ø§Ú©Ù†Ø´â€ŒÙ‡Ø§', icon: Icons.wallet(t.muted), s: 'transactions' as Screen },
    { label: 'ØªÙ†Ø¸ÛŒÙ…Ø§Øª', icon: Icons.settings(t.muted), s: 'settings' as Screen },
    { label: 'ØªÙ…Ø§Ø³ Ø¨Ø§ Ù…Ø§', icon: Icons.phone(t.muted), s: 'contact' as Screen },
  ]

      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60" />
      <div className="absolute rounded-2xl overflow-hidden animate-scalein card-shadow"
        style={{
          background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
          top: '70px', right: '16px', width: '200px',
          transformOrigin: 'top right', maxWidth: 'calc(100vw - 32px)',
        }}
        onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>

const WALLET_ADDRESS = '0x39d8d946d1b9698ca166b21d805ee94f969a188d'

function ReceiveScreen({ phone, onNav, theme }: { phone: string; onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light' }) {

  const [copied, setCopied] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const CARD = '6219-8610-3456-7891'
  const ANPARDAZ_ID = 'Û´Û¸Û²-Û¹Û±Û³-Û¶'

  const copy = (val: string, key: string) => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(val).catch(() => {})
    setCopied(key)
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(null), 2000)
  }

  const Row = ({ label, value, copyKey }: { label: string; value: string; copyKey: string }) => (
    <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl mb-3" style={{ background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <button onClick={() => copy(value, copyKey)}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl transition-all"
          style={{ background: copied === copyKey ? 'rgba(0,200,150,0.15)' : t.card }}>
          {copied === copyKey ? Icons.check() : Icons.copy(t.muted)}

            {copied === copyKey ? 'Ú©Ù¾ÛŒ Ø´Ø¯' : 'Ú©Ù¾ÛŒ'}
          </span>
        </button>
        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-right break-all" style={{ color: t.text }} dir="ltr">{value}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen" style={{ background: t.bg }}>
      <Header title="ÙˆØ§Ø±ÛŒØ² ÙˆØ¬Ù‡" onBack={() => onNav('home')} theme={theme} />

          </p>

// â”€â”€â”€ Transfer â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function TransferScreen({ onNav, theme, usdtPrice, balance }: { onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light'; usdtPrice: number; balance: Balance }) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1)
  const [srcCurrency, setSrcCurrency] = useState<Currency>('rial')
  const [destType, setDestType] = useState<DestType>('kelid')
  const [destValue, setDestValue] = useState('')
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState('')
  const [destCurrency, setDestCurrency] = useState<Currency>('rial')
  const [kelidStatus, setKelidStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'checking' | 'found' | 'notfound'>('idle')
  const [warnAccepted, setWarnAccepted] = useState(false)
  const [paymentId] = useState(() => Math.floor(1000000 + Math.random() * 9000000).toString())
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false)
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)

  const COMMISSION = 0.003
  const NET_FEE_USDT = 1
  const amountNum = parseFloat(amount.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) || 0

  const computeFees = () => {
    if (srcCurrency === 'rial' && destCurrency === 'rial') {
      const fee = Math.round(amountNum * COMMISSION)
      return { deductRial: amountNum + fee, depositRial: amountNum, depositUsdt: 0, feeRial: fee, feeUsdt: 0, netFee: 0 }
    }
    if (srcCurrency === 'usdt' && destCurrency === 'usdt') {
      const fee = amountNum * COMMISSION
      return { deductRial: 0, depositRial: 0, depositUsdt: amountNum - fee - NET_FEE_USDT, feeRial: 0, feeUsdt: fee, netFee: NET_FEE_USDT }
    }
    if (srcCurrency === 'rial' && destCurrency === 'usdt') {
      const usdt = amountNum / usdtPrice
      const fee = usdt * COMMISSION
      return { deductRial: amountNum, depositRial: 0, depositUsdt: usdt - fee - NET_FEE_USDT, feeRial: 0, feeUsdt: fee, netFee: NET_FEE_USDT }
    }
    const rial = amountNum * usdtPrice
    const fee = rial * COMMISSION
    return { deductRial: 0, depositRial: rial - fee, depositUsdt: 0, feeRial: fee, feeUsdt: 0, netFee: 0 }
  }
  const fees = computeFees()

  const checkKelid = useCallback(async () => {
    if (destType !== 'kelid' || !destValue) return
    setKelidStatus('checking')
    try {
      const d = await apiCall(`/api/users/lookup?phone=${destValue}`)
      setKelidStatus(d.found ? 'found' : 'notfound')
    } catch {
      setKelidStatus(KNOWN_USERS.includes(destValue) ? 'found' : 'notfound')
    }
  }, [destType, destValue])

  const handleTransfer = async () => {
    if (amountNum <= 0 || submitting) return
    setSubmitting(true)
    try {
      const d = await apiCall('/api/transfer', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ srcCurrency, destType, destValue, amount: amountNum, destCurrency, paymentId }),
      })
      if (d.success || d.id) { setDone(true); return }
    } catch {}
    setDone(true)
    setSubmitting(false)
  }

  if (done) return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen items-center justify-center px-5 animate-scalein" style={{ background: t.bg }}>
      <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-5"
        style={{ background: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.12)' }}>
        {Icons.check('#00c896')}
      </div>
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: t.text }}>Ø§Ù†ØªÙ‚Ø§Ù„ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚</h2>
      <p className="text-center text-sm mb-2" style={{ color: t.muted }}>
        {srcCurrency === 'rial' ? `${amountNum.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} Ø±ÛŒØ§Ù„` : `${amountNum} USDT`} Ù…Ù†ØªÙ‚Ù„ Ø´Ø¯
      </p>
      <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl text-center mt-2 mb-4" style={{ background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
        <p className="text-xs mb-1" style={{ color: t.muted }}>Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ù‡ Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø®Øª</p>
        <p className="text-[#00c896] font-mono text-xl font-bold">{paymentId}</p>
      </div>
      <button onClick={() => onNav('home')} className="w-full rounded-2xl text-white font-bold mt-4"
        style={{ height: '52px', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #e8512a, #c93e1a)' }}>
        Ø¨Ø§Ø²Ú¯Ø´Øª Ø¨Ù‡ Ø®Ø§Ù†Ù‡
      </button>
    </div>
  )

  const rialBal = balance.loaded ? balance.rial.toLocaleString('fa-IR') : '---'
  const usdtBal = balance.loaded ? balance.usdt.toLocaleString('fa-IR', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) : '---'

// â”€â”€â”€ Transactions â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function TransactionsScreen({ onNav, theme }: { onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light' }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<'all' | 'in' | 'out'>('all')
  const [txList, setTxList] = useState<Transaction[]>(FALLBACK_TRANSACTIONS)
  const t = useTheme(theme)

  useEffect(() => {
    apiCall('/api/transactions')
      .then(d => { if (d.transactions?.length > 0) setTxList(d.transactions) })
      .catch(() => {})
  }, [])

  const filtered = txList.filter(tx => filter === 'all' || tx.type === filter)

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen" style={{ background: t.bg }}>
      <Header title="ØªØ±Ø§Ú©Ù†Ø´â€ŒÙ‡Ø§" onBack={() => onNav('home')} theme={theme} />
      <div className="flex gap-2 px-4 mb-4">
        {[['all', 'Ù‡Ù…Ù‡'], ['in', 'Ø¯Ø±ÛŒØ§ÙØªÛŒ'], ['out', 'Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø®ØªÛŒ']].map(([k, l]) => (
          <button key={k} onClick={() => setFilter(k as typeof filter)}
            className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-all"
            style={{ background: filter === k ? '#00c896' : t.surface, color: filter === k ? 'white' : t.muted, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
            {l}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 pb-24">
        {filtered.map(tx => (
          <div key={tx.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-2xl mb-2"
            style={{ background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0"
              style={{ background: tx.type === 'in' ? 'rgba(0,200,150,0.12)' : 'rgba(232,81,42,0.12)' }}>
              {tx.type === 'in' ? Icons.receive('#00c896') : Icons.transfer('#e8512a')}
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium truncate text-right" style={{ color: t.text }}>{tx.title}</p>
              <p className="text-xs truncate text-right mt-0.5" style={{ color: t.muted }}>{tx.subtitle}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: tx.type === 'in' ? '#00c896' : '#e8512a' }}>
                {tx.type === 'in' ? '+' : '-'}{tx.amount} {tx.currency === 'usdt' ? 'USDT' : 'Ø±ÛŒØ§Ù„'}
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs" style={{ color: t.muted }}>{tx.date}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <BottomNav active="home" onNav={onNav} theme={theme} />
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Settings â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function SettingsScreen({ onNav, theme, setTheme, notif, setNotif }: { onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light'; setTheme: (t: 'dark' | 'light') => void; notif: boolean; setNotif: (v: boolean) => void }) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  const Toggle = ({ value, onChange }: { value: boolean; onChange: (v: boolean) => void }) => (
    <button onClick={() => onChange(!value)} className="w-12 h-6 rounded-full transition-all relative flex-shrink-0"
      style={{ background: value ? '#00c896' : (theme === 'dark' ? '#333' : '#ccc') }}>
      <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-white absolute top-0.5 transition-all"
        style={{ left: value ? '26px' : '2px', boxShadow: '0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.3)' }} />
    </button>
  )
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen" style={{ background: t.bg }}>
      <Header title="ØªÙ†Ø¸ÛŒÙ…Ø§Øª" onBack={() => onNav('home')} theme={theme} />
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 pb-24">
        <p className="text-xs font-medium mb-2 text-right" style={{ color: t.muted }}>Ø¸Ø§Ù‡Ø±</p>
        <div className="rounded-2xl mb-4" style={{ background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-4">
            <Toggle value={theme === 'dark'} onChange={v => setTheme(v ? 'dark' : 'light')} />
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="text-right">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium" style={{ color: t.text }}>ØªÙ… ØªØ§Ø±ÛŒÚ©</p>
                <p className="text-xs" style={{ color: t.muted }}>{theme === 'dark' ? 'ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„' : 'ØºÛŒØ±ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„'}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center" style={{ background: theme === 'dark' ? '#2a2a2a' : '#f0f0f0' }}>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '18px' }}>{theme === 'dark' ? 'ğŸŒ™' : 'â˜€ï¸'}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs font-medium mb-2 text-right" style={{ color: t.muted }}>Ø§Ø¹Ù„Ø§Ù†â€ŒÙ‡Ø§</p>
        <div className="rounded-2xl" style={{ background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-4">
            <Toggle value={notif} onChange={setNotif} />
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="text-right">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium" style={{ color: t.text }}>Ø§Ø¹Ù„Ø§Ù†â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ ØªØ±Ø§Ú©Ù†Ø´</p>
                <p className="text-xs" style={{ color: t.muted }}>{notif ? 'ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„' : 'ØºÛŒØ±ÙØ¹Ø§Ù„'}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center" style={{ background: theme === 'dark' ? '#2a2a2a' : '#f0f0f0' }}>
                {Icons.bell(t.muted)}

            </div>

// â”€â”€â”€ Root â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export default function App() {
  const [screen, setScreen] = useState<Screen>('splash')
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<'dark' | 'light'>('dark')
  const [notif, setNotif] = useState(true)
  const [usdtPrice, setUsdtPrice] = useState(700000)
  const [balance, setBalance] = useState<Balance>({ rial: 0, usdt: 0, loaded: false })

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchPrice = async () => {
      try {
        const d = await apiCall('/api/price/usdt')
        if (d.price) setUsdtPrice(d.price)
      } catch {
        try {
          const r = await fetch('https://api.nobitex.ir/market/stats?srcCurrency=usdt&dstCurrency=rls')
          const d = await r.json()
          const p = d?.stats?.['usdt-rls']?.lastTradePrice
          if (p) setUsdtPrice(parseFloat(p))
        } catch {}
      }
    }
    fetchPrice()
    const iv = setInterval(fetchPrice, 30000)
    return () => clearInterval(iv)
  }, [])

  const fetchBalance = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const d = await apiCall('/api/wallet/balance')
      if (d.rial_balance !== undefined) {
        setBalance({ rial: Number(d.rial_balance), usdt: parseFloat(d.usdt_balance || '0'), loaded: true })
      }
    } catch {
      setBalance(b => ({ ...b, loaded: true }))
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => { if (screen === 'home') fetchBalance() }, [screen, fetchBalance])

  const nav = (s: Screen) => setScreen(s)

  const handleLogin = (p: string) => { setPhone(p); nav('otp') }

  const handleVerify = async (code: string) => {
    try {
      const d = await apiCall('/api/auth/verify-otp', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ phone, code }),
      })
      if (d.token) localStorage.setItem('anpardaz_token', d.token)
    } catch {}
    nav('home')
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-sm mx-auto relative" style={{ minHeight: '100vh', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
      {screen === 'splash' && <SplashScreen onDone={() => nav('login')} />}
      {screen === 'login' && <LoginScreen onLogin={handleLogin} />}
      {screen === 'otp' && <OtpScreen phone={phone} onVerify={handleVerify} />}
      {screen === 'home' && <HomeScreen phone={phone || '09137654321'} onNav={nav} theme={theme} notif={notif} balance={balance} />}
      {screen === 'transfer' && <TransferScreen onNav={nav} theme={theme} usdtPrice={usdtPrice} balance={balance} />}
      {screen === 'receive' && <ReceiveScreen phone={phone || '09137654321'} onNav={nav} theme={theme} />}
      {screen === 'transactions' && <TransactionsScreen onNav={nav} theme={theme} />}
      {screen === 'internet' && <InternetScreen onNav={nav} theme={theme} />}
      {screen === 'settings' && <SettingsScreen onNav={nav} theme={theme} setTheme={setTheme} notif={notif} setNotif={setNotif} />}
      {screen === 'addcard' && <AddCardScreen onNav={nav} theme={theme} />}
      {screen === 'contact' && <ContactScreen onNav={nav} theme={theme} />}
    </div>
  )
}

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react'
import logoMark from './imports/25087b17-7744-4c8c-b6b5-85499fc1d7bc.jpg'

// â”€â”€â”€ API â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://78.157.51.76/api'
const APP_NAME = 'Ø¢Ù† Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø²'

async function apiCall(path: string, opts: RequestInit = {}) {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('anpardaz_token')
  const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    ...opts,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      ...(opts.headers as Record<string, string> || {}),
    },
  })
  return res.json()
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Theme helpers â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function useTheme(theme: 'dark' | 'light') {
  return {
    bg: theme === 'dark' ? '#0f0f0f' : '#f2f4f7',
    surface: theme === 'dark' ? '#1a1a1a' : '#ffffff',
    card: theme === 'dark' ? '#222222' : '#ffffff',
    border: theme === 'dark' ? '#2e2e2e' : '#e2e6ea',
    text: theme === 'dark' ? '#f0f0f0' : '#111827',
    muted: theme === 'dark' ? '#888888' : '#6b7280',
    sub: theme === 'dark' ? '#555' : '#9ca3af',
  }
}

// â”€â”€â”€ App Logo â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function LogoMark({ size = 36 }: { size?: number }) {
  return (
    <img src={logoMark} alt={APP_NAME}
      style={{ width: size, height: size, borderRadius: size * 0.22, objectFit: 'cover' }} />
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Header â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function Header({
  title, onBack, onMenu, showBell, theme, notif
}: {
  title?: string; onBack?: () => void; onMenu?: () => void
  showBell?: boolean; theme: 'dark' | 'light'; notif?: boolean
}) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 pt-4 pb-3 flex-shrink-0">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        {showBell && (
          <button className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center relative"
            style={{ background: t.surface }}>
            {Icons.bell(t.muted)}
            {notif && <span className="absolute top-1.5 right-1.5 w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#e8512a]" />}
          </button>
        )}
        {onMenu && (
          <button onClick={onMenu} className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center"
            style={{ background: t.surface }}>
            {Icons.menu(t.muted)}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      {title ? (
        <span className="text-base font-bold" style={{ color: t.text }}>{title}</span>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <LogoMark size={28} />
          <span className="text-base font-bold" style={{ color: t.text }}>{APP_NAME}</span>
        </div>
      )}
      {onBack ? (
        <button onClick={onBack} className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center"
          style={{ background: t.surface }}>
          {Icons.arrowRight(t.muted)}
        </button>
      ) : <div className="w-9" />}
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Bottom Nav â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function BottomNav({ active, onNav, theme }: { active: Screen; onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light' }) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  const tabs = [
    { s: 'contact' as Screen, label: 'ØªÙ…Ø§Ø³ Ø¨Ø§ Ù…Ø§', icon: (a: boolean) => Icons.phone(a ? '#00c896' : t.muted) },
    { s: 'settings' as Screen, label: 'ØªÙ†Ø¸ÛŒÙ…Ø§Øª', icon: (a: boolean) => Icons.settings(a ? '#00c896' : t.muted) },
    { s: 'home' as Screen, label: 'ØµÙØ­Ù‡ Ø§ØµÙ„ÛŒ', icon: (a: boolean) => Icons.home(a ? '#00c896' : t.muted) },
  ]
  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40"
      style={{ background: t.surface, borderTop: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-around py-2 px-4 max-w-sm mx-auto">
        {tabs.map(tab => (
          <button key={tab.s} onClick={() => onNav(tab.s)}
            className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1 px-4 py-1 rounded-xl transition-all">
            {tab.icon(active === tab.s)}
            <span className="text-[10px] font-medium"
              style={{ color: active === tab.s ? '#00c896' : t.muted }}>
              {tab.label}
            </span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Splash â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function SplashScreen({ onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState(0)
  useEffect(() => {
    const t1 = setTimeout(() => setPhase(1), 200)
    const t2 = setTimeout(() => setPhase(2), 900)
    const t3 = setTimeout(() => setPhase(3), 1600)
    const t4 = setTimeout(() => onDone(), 2400)
    return () => { clearTimeout(t1); clearTimeout(t2); clearTimeout(t3); clearTimeout(t4) }
  }, [onDone])

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden"
      style={{ background: '#050505' }}>
      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center pointer-events-none">
        <div className="w-80 h-80 rounded-full"
          style={{
            background: 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(0,200,150,0.08) 0%, transparent 70%)',
            opacity: phase >= 1 ? 1 : 0,
            transition: 'opacity 0.8s ease',
          }} />
      </div>

      <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-center"
        style={{
          opacity: phase >= 1 ? 1 : 0,
          transform: phase >= 1 ? 'scale(1) translateY(0)' : 'scale(0.75) translateY(20px)',
          transition: 'all 0.8s cubic-bezier(0.34,1.2,0.64,1)',
        }}>
        <img src={logoFull} alt={APP_NAME}
          style={{ width: 220, height: 220, objectFit: 'contain' }} />
      </div>

      <div className="relative z-10 mt-2"
        style={{
          opacity: phase >= 2 ? 1 : 0,
          transform: phase >= 2 ? 'translateY(0)' : 'translateY(12px)',
          transition: 'all 0.5s ease',
        }}>
        <p className="text-sm font-medium tracking-wide" style={{ color: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.7)' }}>
          Ú©ÛŒÙ Ù¾ÙˆÙ„ Ø¯ÛŒØ¬ÛŒØªØ§Ù„ Ø´Ù…Ø§
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="absolute bottom-14 flex flex-col items-center gap-3"
        style={{ opacity: phase >= 3 ? 1 : 0, transition: 'all 0.4s ease' }}>
        <div className="flex gap-1.5">
          {[0, 1, 2].map(i => (
            <div key={i} className="rounded-full bg-[#00c896]"
              style={{ width: i === 1 ? '18px' : '6px', height: '6px', opacity: 0.4 + i * 0.3 }} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ Login â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function LoginScreen({ onLogin }: { onLogin: (p: string) => void }) {
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
  const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    if (phone.length < 10 || loading) return
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      await apiCall('/api/auth/send-otp', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ phone }) })
    } catch {}
    setLoading(false)
    onLogin(phone)
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden" style={{ background: '#050505', height: '100dvh' }}>
      {/* Logo */}
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-8 pb-3 px-6 flex-shrink-0">
        <img src={logoFull} alt={APP_NAME}
          style={{ width: 120, height: 120, objectFit: 'contain' }} />
        <p className="text-[#00c896] text-xs font-medium tracking-wide mt-1">Ú©ÛŒÙ Ù¾ÙˆÙ„ Ø¯ÛŒØ¬ÛŒØªØ§Ù„</p>
      </div>

      <div className="h-px mx-8 flex-shrink-0" style={{ background: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.12)' }} />

      {/* Form */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col px-5 pt-5 pb-5 overflow-hidden">
        <h2 className="text-white text-sm font-semibold text-right mb-3 flex-shrink-0">Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡ Ù‡Ù…Ø±Ø§Ù‡ Ø®ÙˆØ¯ Ø±Ø§ ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ú©Ù†ÛŒØ¯</h2>

        <div className="mb-3 flex-shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 rounded-2xl h-13 transition-all"
            style={{
              height: '52px',
              background: '#1a1a1a',
              border: `2px solid ${focused ? '#00c896' : '#2e2e2e'}`,
              boxShadow: focused ? '0 0 0 4px rgba(0,200,150,0.08)' : 'none',
            }}>
            <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0" style={{ background: '#1e3030' }}>
              {Icons.mobile('#00c896')}
            </div>
            <input type="tel" value={phone}
              onChange={e => setPhone(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 11))}
              onFocus={() => setFocused(true)} onBlur={() => setFocused(false)}
              onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && handleSubmit()}
              placeholder="09xxxxxxxxx" dir="ltr"
              className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-white placeholder-[#444] text-base outline-none text-right"
              style={{ fontFamily: 'Vazirmatn, sans-serif' }} />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-xl mb-3 flex-shrink-0"
          style={{ background: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.06)', border: '1px solid rgba(0,200,150,0.12)' }}>
          {Icons.info('#00c896')}
          <p className="text-[#6b9e8e] text-xs text-right flex-1">Ú©Ø¯ ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡ Ø´Ù…Ø§ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØ´ÙˆØ¯</p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 min-h-0" />

        <p className="text-[#444] text-xs text-center mb-3 leading-relaxed flex-shrink-0">
          ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯ Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…Ø¹Ù†Ø§ÛŒ Ù¾Ø°ÛŒØ±Ø´ <span className="text-[#00c896]">Ù‚ÙˆØ§Ù†ÛŒÙ†</span> Ùˆ <span className="text-[#00c896]">Ø­Ø±ÛŒÙ… Ø®ØµÙˆØµÛŒ</span> {APP_NAME} Ø§Ø³Øª
        </p>
        <button onClick={handleSubmit}
          disabled={phone.length < 10 || loading}
          className="w-full rounded-2xl text-white font-bold text-sm transition-all flex-shrink-0"
          style={{
            height: '52px',
            background: phone.length >= 10 && !loading
              ? 'linear-gradient(135deg, #e8512a, #c93e1a)'
              : '#1a1a1a',
            color: phone.length >= 10 && !loading ? 'white' : '#444',
          }}>
          {loading ? 'Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ Ø§Ø±Ø³Ø§Ù„...' : 'Ø§Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù‡'}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// â”€â”€â”€ OTP â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function OtpScreen({ phone, onVerify }: { phone: string; onVerify: (code: string) => void }) {
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState(['', '', '', '', ''])
  const [timer, setTimer] = useState(90)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const inputRefs = useRef<(HTMLInputElement | null)[]>([null, null, null, null, null])

  useEffect(() => { inputRefs.current[0]?.focus() }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    const iv = setInterval(() => setTimer(x => x > 0 ? x - 1 : 0), 1000)
    return () => clearInterval(iv)
  }, [])

  const handleChange = (i: number, v: string) => {
    if (!/^\d*$/.test(v)) return
    const digit = v.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-1)
    const next = [...otp]
    next[i] = digit
    setOtp(next)
    if (digit && i < 4) inputRefs.current[i + 1]?.focus()
    if (next.every(d => d)) setTimeout(() => onVerify(next.join('')), 300)
  }

  const handleKeyDown = (i: number, e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Backspace' && !otp[i] && i > 0) {
      const next = [...otp]; next[i - 1] = ''
      setOtp(next)
      inputRefs.current[i - 1]?.focus()
    }
  }

    setTimer(90)
    try { await apiCall('/api/auth/send-otp', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ phone }) }) } catch {}
  }

    <div className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden" style={{ background: '#050505', height: '100dvh' }}>
      <Header title="ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ø´Ù…Ø§Ø±Ù‡" onBack={() => {}} theme="dark" />

              ref={el => { inputRefs.current[i] = el }}
              type="tel" inputMode="numeric" value={d}
              onChange={e => handleChange(i, e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={e => handleKeyDown(i, e)}
              maxLength={1}

          {loading ? 'Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ Ø¨Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒ...' : 'ØªØ§ÛŒÛŒØ¯ Ùˆ ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯'}

}

function DropdownMenu({ onClose, onNav, theme }: { onClose: () => void; onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light' }) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  const items = [
    { label: 'Ù¾Ø±ÙˆÙØ§ÛŒÙ„', icon: Icons.user(t.muted), s: 'home' as Screen },

    { label: 'ØªØ±Ø§Ú©Ù†Ø´â€ŒÙ‡Ø§', icon: Icons.wallet(t.muted), s: 'transactions' as Screen },

      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60" />
      <div className="absolute rounded-2xl overflow-hidden animate-scalein card-shadow"
        style={{
          background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}`,
          top: '70px', right: '16px', width: '200px',

const WALLET_ADDRESS = '0x39d8d946d1b9698ca166b21d805ee94f969a188d'

function ReceiveScreen({ phone, onNav, theme }: { phone: string; onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light' }) {

    navigator.clipboard.writeText(val).catch(() => {})

    setTimeout(() => setCopied(null), 2000)
  }

          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl transition-all"
          style={{ background: copied === copyKey ? 'rgba(0,200,150,0.15)' : t.card }}>

      <Header title="ÙˆØ§Ø±ÛŒØ² ÙˆØ¬Ù‡" onBack={() => onNav('home')} theme={theme} />

// â”€â”€â”€ Transfer â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function TransferScreen({ onNav, theme, usdtPrice, balance }: { onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light'; usdtPrice: number; balance: Balance }) {
  const t = useTheme(theme)
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1)
  const [srcCurrency, setSrcCurrency] = useState<Currency>('rial')
  const [destType, setDestType] = useState<DestType>('kelid')
  const [destValue, setDestValue] = useState('')
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState('')
  const [destCurrency, setDestCurrency] = useState<Currency>('rial')
  const [kelidStatus, setKelidStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'checking' | 'found' | 'notfound'>('idle')
  const [warnAccepted, setWarnAccepted] = useState(false)
  const [paymentId] = useState(() => Math.floor(1000000 + Math.random() * 9000000).toString())
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false)
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)

  const COMMISSION = 0.003
  const NET_FEE_USDT = 1
  const amountNum = parseFloat(amount.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) || 0

  const computeFees = () => {
    if (srcCurrency === 'rial' && destCurrency === 'rial') {
      const fee = Math.round(amountNum * COMMISSION)
      return { deductRial: amountNum + fee, depositRial: amountNum, depositUsdt: 0, feeRial: fee, feeUsdt: 0, netFee: 0 }
    }
    if (srcCurrency === 'usdt' && destCurrency === 'usdt') {
      const fee = amountNum * COMMISSION
      return { deductRial: 0, depositRial: 0, depositUsdt: amountNum - fee - NET_FEE_USDT, feeRial: 0, feeUsdt: fee, netFee: NET_FEE_USDT }
    }
    if (srcCurrency === 'rial' && destCurrency === 'usdt') {
      const usdt = amountNum / usdtPrice
      const fee = usdt * COMMISSION
      return { deductRial: amountNum, depositRial: 0, depositUsdt: usdt - fee - NET_FEE_USDT, feeRial: 0, feeUsdt: fee, netFee: NET_FEE_USDT }
    }
    const rial = amountNum * usdtPrice
    const fee = rial * COMMISSION
    return { deductRial: 0, depositRial: rial - fee, depositUsdt: 0, feeRial: fee, feeUsdt: 0, netFee: 0 }
  }
  const fees = computeFees()

  const checkKelid = useCallback(async () => {
    if (destType !== 'kelid' || !destValue) return
    setKelidStatus('checking')
    try {
      const d = await apiCall(`/api/users/lookup?phone=${destValue}`)
      setKelidStatus(d.found ? 'found' : 'notfound')
    } catch {
      setKelidStatus(KNOWN_USERS.includes(destValue) ? 'found' : 'notfound')
    }
  }, [destType, destValue])

  const handleTransfer = async () => {
    if (amountNum <= 0 || submitting) return
    setSubmitting(true)
    try {
      const d = await apiCall('/api/transfer', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ srcCurrency, destType, destValue, amount: amountNum, destCurrency, paymentId }),
      })
      if (d.success || d.id) { setDone(true); return }
    } catch {}
    setDone(true)
    setSubmitting(false)
  }

  if (done) return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen items-center justify-center px-5 animate-scalein" style={{ background: t.bg }}>
      <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-5"
        style={{ background: 'rgba(0,200,150,0.12)' }}>
        {Icons.check('#00c896')}
      </div>
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: t.text }}>Ø§Ù†ØªÙ‚Ø§Ù„ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚</h2>
      <p className="text-center text-sm mb-2" style={{ color: t.muted }}>
        {srcCurrency === 'rial' ? `${amountNum.toLocaleString('fa-IR')} Ø±ÛŒØ§Ù„` : `${amountNum} USDT`} Ù…Ù†ØªÙ‚Ù„ Ø´Ø¯
      </p>
      <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl text-center mt-2 mb-4" style={{ background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
        <p className="text-xs mb-1" style={{ color: t.muted }}>Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ù‡ Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø®Øª</p>
        <p className="text-[#00c896] font-mono text-xl font-bold">{paymentId}</p>

// â”€â”€â”€ Transactions â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
function TransactionsScreen({ onNav, theme }: { onNav: (s: Screen) => void; theme: 'dark' | 'light' }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<'all' | 'in' | 'out'>('all')
  const [txList, setTxList] = useState<Transaction[]>(FALLBACK_TRANSACTIONS)
  const t = useTheme(theme)

  useEffect(() => {
    apiCall('/api/transactions')
      .then(d => { if (d.transactions?.length > 0) setTxList(d.transactions) })
      .catch(() => {})
  }, [])

  const filtered = txList.filter(tx => filter === 'all' || tx.type === filter)

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-screen" style={{ background: t.bg }}>
      <Header title="ØªØ±Ø§Ú©Ù†Ø´â€ŒÙ‡Ø§" onBack={() => onNav('home')} theme={theme} />
      <div className="flex gap-2 px-4 mb-4">
        {[['all', 'Ù‡Ù…Ù‡'], ['in', 'Ø¯Ø±ÛŒØ§ÙØªÛŒ'], ['out', 'Ù¾Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø®ØªÛŒ']].map(([k, l]) => (
          <button key={k} onClick={() => setFilter(k as typeof filter)}
            className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium transition-all"
            style={{ background: filter === k ? '#00c896' : t.surface, color: filter === k ? 'white' : t.muted, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
            {l}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 pb-24">
        {filtered.map(tx => (
          <div key={tx.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-2xl mb-2"
            style={{ background: t.surface, border: `1px solid ${t.border}` }}>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0"
              style={{ background: tx.type === 'in' ? 'rgba(0,200,150,0.12)' : 'rgba(232,81,42,0.12)' }}>
              {tx.type === 'in' ? Icons.receive('#00c896') : Icons.transfer('#e8512a')}
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium truncate text-right" style={{ color: t.text }}>{tx.title}</p>
              <p className="text-xs truncate text-right mt-0.5" style={{ color: t.muted }}>{tx.subtitle}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right flex-shrink-0">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: tx.type === 'in' ? '#00c896' : '#e8512a' }}>
                {tx.type === 'in' ? '+' : '-'}{tx.amount} {tx.currency === 'usdt' ? 'USDT' : 'Ø±ÛŒØ§Ù„'}
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs" style={{ color: t.muted }}>{tx.date}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <BottomNav active="home" onNav={onNav} theme={theme} />
    </div>
  )
}
